# Seed 56 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small; the first run checked on four machines (Iteration 354)

`seed 56 tag2 10h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier --cpus 2,8`, commit 5231a9ef7
(Iteration 351) - the after-seed-52 genes a fifth time. The chain 02:48-12:25 UTC Oct 17 (9 h 37 min):
the evolution about 7 h, the re-measure 23 min, the comparison at 6 rounds 1 h 18 min. 44,356 designs.

The verdict: fastest -4.1%, smallest +0.04%, memory +0 KB, hypervolume +1.3%, best Q -0.9% - **small**,
the fourth in a row of these genes. bfind on the front: 0 112, 1 75. No corpus deaths; children: mutation
52.5%, crossover 36.3%, borrowed 11.2%; the JIT on 111 of the front's 187. (No predictions were
written for this run.)

**The checks - all four gathered** (results/xcheck-seed56.md):
- **rage.lan, the Tegra (ARMv7, native, 4-byte cells, gcc 16) - its first: 12 designs, Spearman 0.944**;
  the laptop's six fastest 1 4 3 2 5 7 there. The laptop's ranking carries to another architecture.
- dream.lan 64-bit: 0.917 (the six fastest 1 5 4 6 3 9).
- dream.lan 32-bit x86: 0.833 (5 8 7 10 9 2); **its pair, the same 24 designs at 64 bits: 0.977** (1 3
  2 4 5 7) - on one machine, 64 against 32 bits: Spearman 0.854, r = 32/64 median 1.06 (0.93-1.76).
  **The skew is the width's, not the machine's.**
- **fsys 3 keeps its place at 32 bits on real hardware: r 1.00 over 12 designs** - the VM's 2x was
  its own 32-bit system calls (Iteration 353). The losers are one lineage: the five fastest at 64 bits
  (0.38-0.42), r 1.67-1.76 - all with klookup, kinput, rtimm 2, swapi, fsys 2, spec with var, no
  regpin; the rest have none of those. Candidates, not causes: the C kernel primitives first.
- Held out (heldout.py):
  sieve (held out): 187 designs; Spearman with the selection speed 0.963
    the 10 fastest by selection, their rank on it: 3 4 5 6 10 1 2 7 8 46

---

# Evolved VM designs

32095 designs evaluated, 31900 alive, 12261 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): be27764d7a, speed 0.134, binary + image 28,178, memory 168 KB - Q 0.194 of s6-cv8b's.

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
